Je veux être enterré dans leur corbillard.\nJe refuse l&apos;oraison de toutes les Églises. Je demande une prière à toutes les âmes.\nJe crois en Dieu.\n&lt;/poem>"}},"i":0}}]}'/><blockquote class="templatequote " about="#mwt350" id="mwA3w"><div lang="fr" style="margin-center: 1em; font-style: italic;" class="poem" typeof="mw:Extension/poem" about="#mwt352" id="mwA30" data-mw='{"name":"poem","attrs":{"lang":"fr","style":"margin-center: 1em; font-style: italic;"},"body":{"extsrc":"\nJe donne cinquante mille francs aux pauvres. Je veux être enterré dans leur corbillard.\nJe refuse l&apos;oraison de toutes les Églises. Je demande une prière à toutes les âmes.\nJe crois en Dieu.\n"}}'><p id="mwA34">Je donne cinquante mille francs aux pauvres. Je veux être enterré dans leur corbillard.<br id="mwA38"/>
Je refuse l'oraison de toutes les Églises. Je demande une prière à toutes les âmes.<br id="mwA4A"/>
Je crois en Dieu.</p></div></blockquote>
<link rel="mw-deduplicated-inline-style" href="mw-data:TemplateStyles:r1244412712" about="#mwt353" typeof="mw:Extension/templatestyles mw:Transclusion" id="mwA4E" data-mw='{"name":"templatestyles","attrs":{"src":"Template:Blockquote/styles.css"},"parts":[{"template":{"target":{"wt":"blockquote","href":"./Template:Blockquote"},"params":{"1":{"wt":"&lt;poem lang=\"fr\" style=\"margin-center: 1em; font-style:;\">\nI leave 50,000 francs to the poor. I wish to be buried in their hearse.\nI refuse [funeral] orations from all Churches. I ask [for] a prayer to all souls.\nI believe in God.\n&lt;/poem>"}},"i":0}}]}'/><blockquote class="templatequote " about="#mwt353" id="mwA4I"><div lang="fr" style="margin-center: 1em; font-style:;" class="poem" typeof="mw:Extension/poem" about="#mwt355" id="mwA4M" data-mw='{"name":"poem","attrs":{"lang":"fr","style":"margin-center: 1em; font-style:;"},"body":{"extsrc":"\nI leave 50,000 francs to the poor. I wish to be buried in their hearse.\nI refuse [funeral] orations from all Churches. I ask [for] a prayer to all souls.\nI believe in God.\n"}}'><p id="mwA4Q">I leave 50,000 francs to the poor. I wish to be buried in their hearse.<br id="mwA4U"/>
I refuse [funeral] orations from all Churches. I ask [for] a prayer to all souls.<br id="mwA4Y"/>
I believe in God.</p></div></blockquote>

</section><section data-mw-section-id="7" id="mwA4c" aria-labelledby="Drawings"><div class="mw-heading mw-heading2"><h2 id="Drawings">Drawings</h2><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=Victor_Hugo&amp;action=edit&amp;section=7" title="Edit section: Drawings"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<p id="mwA4g">Hugo produced more than 4,000 drawings, with about 3,000 drawings still in existence. Originally pursued as a casual hobby, drawing became more important to Hugo shortly before his exile when he made the decision to stop writing to devote himself to politics. Drawing became his exclusive creative outlet between 1848 and 1851.<sup about="#mwt358" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-78" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-78"}}'><a href="#cite_note-78" id="mwA4k"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwA4o"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwA4s">[</span>77<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwA4w">]</span></span></a></sup><sup about="#mwt361" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-79" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-79"}}'><a href="#cite_note-79" id="mwA40"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwA44"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwA48">[</span>78<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwA5A">]</span></span></a></sup></p>

<p id="mwA5E">Hugo worked only on paper, and on a small scale; usually in dark brown or black <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Pen_and_ink" title="Pen and ink" class="mw-redirect" id="mwA5I">pen-and-ink</a> wash, sometimes with touches of white, and rarely with colour.<sup about="#mwt364" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-80" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-80"}}'><a href="#cite_note-80" id="mwA5M"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwA5Q"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwA5U">[</span>79<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwA5Y">]</span></span></a></sup></p>

</section><section data-mw-section-id="8" id="mwA5c" aria-labelledby="Personal_life"><div class="mw-heading mw-heading2"><h2 id="Personal_life">Personal life</h2><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=Victor_Hugo&amp;action=edit&amp;section=8" title="Edit section: Personal life"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<section data-mw-section-id="9" id="mwA5g" aria-labelledby="Family"><div class="mw-heading mw-heading3"><h3 id="Family">Family</h3><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=Victor_Hugo&amp;action=edit&amp;section=9" title="Edit section: Family"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<figure class="mw-default-size mw-halign-right" typeof="mw:File/Thumb" id="mwA5k"><a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/File:Châtillon,_Auguste_de_-_Portrait_de_Victor_Hugo_avec_son_fils_de_François-Victor_Hugo_-_194_-_Maison_de_Victor_Hugo.jpg" class="mw-file-description" id="mwA5o"><img resource="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/File:Châtillon,_Auguste_de_-_Portrait_de_Victor_Hugo_avec_son_fils_de_François-Victor_Hugo_-_194_-_Maison_de_Victor_Hugo.jpg" src="//upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/3/36/Ch%C3%A2tillon%2C_Auguste_de_-_Portrait_de_Victor_Hugo_avec_son_fils_de_Fran%C3%A7ois-Victor_Hugo_-_194_-_Maison_de_Victor_Hugo.jpg/250px-Ch%C3%A2tillon%2C_Auguste_de_-_Portrait_de_Victor_Hugo_avec_son_fils_de_Fran%C3%A7ois-Victor_Hugo_-_194_-_Maison_de_Victor_Hugo.jpg" decoding="async" data-file-width="2622" data-file-height="4986" data-file-type="bitmap" height="475" width="250" srcset="//upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/3/36/Ch%C3%A2tillon%2C_Auguste_de_-_Portrait_de_Victor_Hugo_avec_son_fils_de_Fran%C3%A7ois-Victor_Hugo_-_194_-_Maison_de_Victor_Hugo.jpg/500px-Ch%C3%A2tillon%2C_Auguste_de_-_Portrait_de_Victor_Hugo_avec_son_fils_de_Fran%C3%A7ois-Victor_Hugo_-_194_-_Maison_de_Victor_Hugo.jpg 2x" class="mw-file-element" id="mwA5s"/></a><figcaption id="mwA5w">Victor Hugo with his son François-Victor by <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Auguste_de_Châtillon" title="Auguste de Châtillon" id="mwA50">Auguste de Châtillon</a>, 1836</figcaption></figure>
<figure class="mw-default-size mw-halign-right" typeof="mw:File/Thumb" id="mwA54"><a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/File:Victor_Hugo_et_ses_petits-enfants_Georges_et_Jeanne.jpg" class="mw-file-description" id="mwA58"><img resource="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/File:Victor_Hugo_et_ses_petits-enfants_Georges_et_Jeanne.jpg" src="//upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/f/fa/Victor_Hugo_et_ses_petits-enfants_Georges_et_Jeanne.jpg/250px-Victor_Hugo_et_ses_petits-enfants_Georges_et_Jeanne.jpg" decoding="async" data-file-width="1744" data-file-height="2080" data-file-type="bitmap" height="298" width="250" srcset="//upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/f/fa/Victor_Hugo_et_ses_petits-enfants_Georges_et_Jeanne.jpg/500px-Victor_Hugo_et_ses_petits-enfants_Georges_et_Jeanne.jpg 2x" class="mw-file-element" id="mwA6A"/></a><figcaption id="mwA6E">Hugo with his grandchildren <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Jeanne_Hugo" title="Jeanne Hugo" id="mwA6I">Jeanne</a> and Georges, 1881</figcaption></figure>

<section data-mw-section-id="10" id="mwA6M" aria-labelledby="Marriage"><div class="mw-heading mw-heading4"><h4 id="Marriage">Marriage</h4><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=Victor_Hugo&amp;action=edit&amp;section=10" title="Edit section: Marriage"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<p id="mwA6Q">Hugo married <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Adèle_Foucher" title="Adèle Foucher" id="mwA6U">Adèle Foucher</a> in October 1822. Despite their respective affairs, they lived together for nearly 46 years until she died in August 1868. Hugo, who was still banished from France, was unable to attend her funeral in Villequier, where their daughter Léopoldine was buried. From 1830 to 1837, Adèle had an affair with <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Charles_Augustin_Sainte-Beuve" title="Charles Augustin Sainte-Beuve" id="mwA6Y">Charles-Augustin Sainte Beuve</a>, a reviewer and writer.<sup about="#mwt367" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-81" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-81"}}'><a href="#cite_note-81" id="mwA6c"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwA6g"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwA6k">[</span>80<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwA6o">]</span></span></a></sup></p>

</section><section data-mw-section-id="11" id="mwA6s" aria-labelledby="Children"><div class="mw-heading mw-heading4"><h4 id="Children">Children</h4><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=Victor_Hugo&amp;action=edit&amp;section=11" title="Edit section: Children"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<p id="mwA6w">Adèle and Victor Hugo had their first child, Léopold, in 1823, but the boy died in infancy. On 28 August 1824, the couple's second child, <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Léopoldine_Hugo" title="Léopoldine Hugo" id="mwA60">Léopoldine</a>, was born, followed by <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Charles_Hugo_(writer)" title="Charles Hugo (writer)" id="mwA64">Charles</a> on 4 November 1826, <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/François-Victor_Hugo" title="François-Victor Hugo" id="mwA68">François-Victor</a> on 28 October 1828, and <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Adèle_Hugo" title="Adèle Hugo" id="mwA7A">Adèle</a> on 28 July 1830.</p>

<p id="mwA7E">Hugo's eldest and favourite daughter, Léopoldine, died in 1843 at the age of 19, shortly after her marriage to Charles Vacquerie. On 4 September, she drowned in the <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Seine" title="Seine" id="mwA7I">Seine</a> at <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Villequier" title="Villequier" id="mwA7M">Villequier</a> when the boat she was in overturned. Her young husband died trying to save her. The death left her father devastated; Hugo was travelling at the time, in the south of France, when he first learned about Léopoldine's death from a newspaper that he read in a café.<sup about="#mwt370" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-82" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-82"}}'><a href="#cite_note-82" id="mwA7Q"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwA7U"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwA7Y">[</span>81<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwA7c">]</span></span></a></sup></p>
<figure class="mw-default-size" typeof="mw:File/Thumb" id="mwA7g"><a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/File:Léopoldine_lisant,_Adèle_Foucher,_1837,_dessin,_19,2_x_27_cm_,_Maison_de_Victor_Hugo,_Paris.png" class="mw-file-description" id="mwA7k"><img resource="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/File:Léopoldine_lisant,_Adèle_Foucher,_1837,_dessin,_19,2_x_27_cm_,_Maison_de_Victor_Hugo,_Paris.png" src="//upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/e/eb/L%C3%A9opoldine_lisant%2C_Ad%C3%A8le_Foucher%2C_1837%2C_dessin%2C_19%2C2_x_27_cm_%2C_Maison_de_Victor_Hugo%2C_Paris.png/250px-L%C3%A9opoldine_lisant%2C_Ad%C3%A8le_Foucher%2C_1837%2C_dessin%2C_19%2C2_x_27_cm_%2C_Maison_de_Victor_Hugo%2C_Paris.png" decoding="async" data-file-width="3534" data-file-height="2516" data-file-type="bitmap" height="178" width="250" srcset="//upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/e/eb/L%C3%A9opoldine_lisant%2C_Ad%C3%A8le_Foucher%2C_1837%2C_dessin%2C_19%2C2_x_27_cm_%2C_Maison_de_Victor_Hugo%2C_Paris.png/500px-L%C3%A9opoldine_lisant%2C_Ad%C3%A8le_Foucher%2C_1837%2C_dessin%2C_19%2C2_x_27_cm_%2C_Maison_de_Victor_Hugo%2C_Paris.png 2x" class="mw-file-element" id="mwA7o"/></a><figcaption id="mwA7s">Léopoldine reading. Drawing by her mother <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Adèle_Foucher" title="Adèle Foucher" id="mwA7w">Adèle Foucher</a>, 1837</figcaption></figure>
<p about="#mwt371" typeof="mw:Transclusion" id="mwA70" data-mw='{"parts":["He describes his shock and grief in his poem \"À Villequier\":\n",{"template":{"target":{"wt":"Verse translation","href":"./Template:Verse_translation"},"params":{"lang":{"wt":"frm"},"1":{"wt":"Hélas ! vers le passé tournant un œil d&apos;envie,\nSans que rien ici-bas puisse m&apos;en consoler,\nJe regarde toujours ce moment de ma vie\nOù je l&apos;ai vue ouvrir son aile et s&apos;envoler!\n\nJe verrai cet instant jusqu&apos;à ce que je meure,\nL&apos;instant, pleurs superflus !\nOù je criai : L&apos;enfant que j&apos;avais tout à l&apos;heure,\nQuoi donc ! je ne l&apos;ai plus !\n\n"},"2":{"wt":"Alas! My envious eye, turning toward the past,\nWithout anything else down here able to console me,\nI look continually at that moment of my life\nWhere I saw her spread her wings and fly away!\n\nI will see that instant until I die,\nThat instant, nothing beyond!\nWhere I cried: \"The child I had a short time ago,\nWhat! I have her no more!\"\n"}},"i":0}}]}'>He describes his shock and grief in his poem "À Villequier":
<span class="mw-empty-elt"><style data-mw-deduplicate="TemplateStyles:r1319360790" typeof="mw:Extension/templatestyles" about="#mwt372" data-mw='{"name":"templatestyles","attrs":{"src":"Verse translation/styles.css"}}'>.mw-parser-output .verse-translation{margin-left:1em}.mw-parser-output .verse-translation-head{text-align:center;font-weight:bold}.mw-parser-output .verse-translation-blockquote blockquote{margin:0;padding:0;border:0}.mw-parser-output .verse-translation-attr{font-size:90%}@media(min-width:640px){.mw-parser-output .verse-translation-blockquote{display:table-cell;vertical-align:top}.mw-parser-output .verse-translation-blockquote.first{padding-right:2em}}</style></span></p><span about="#mwt371">
</span><div class="verse-translation" about="#mwt371" id="mwA74">
<div class="verse-translation-blockquote first"><blockquote><div style="font-style:italic;text-align:left" lang="frm" class="poem" typeof="mw:Extension/poem" about="#mwt373" id="mwA78" data-mw='{"name":"poem","attrs":{"style":"font-style:italic;text-align:left","lang":"frm"},"body":{"extsrc":"Hélas ! vers le passé tournant un œil d&apos;envie,\nSans que rien ici-bas puisse m&apos;en consoler,\nJe regarde toujours ce moment de ma vie\nOù je l&apos;ai vue ouvrir son aile et s&apos;envoler!\n\nJe verrai cet instant jusqu&apos;à ce que je meure,\nL&apos;instant, pleurs superflus !\nOù je criai : L&apos;enfant que j&apos;avais tout à l&apos;heure,\nQuoi donc ! je ne l&apos;ai plus !\n\n"}}'><p id="mwA8A">Hélas<span typeof="mw:DisplaySpace" id="mwA8E"> </span>! vers le passé tournant un œil d'envie,<br id="mwA8I"/>
Sans que rien ici-bas puisse m'en consoler,<br id="mwA8M"/>
Je regarde toujours ce moment de ma vie<br id="mwA8Q"/>
Où je l'ai vue ouvrir son aile et s'envoler!<br id="mwA8U"/>
<br id="mwA8Y"/>
Je verrai cet instant jusqu'à ce que je meure,<br id="mwA8c"/>
L'instant, pleurs superflus<span typeof="mw:DisplaySpace" id="mwA8g"> </span>!<br id="mwA8k"/>
Où je criai<span typeof="mw:DisplaySpace" id="mwA8o"> </span>: L'enfant que j'avais tout à l'heure,<br id="mwA8s"/>
Quoi donc<span typeof="mw:DisplaySpace" id="mwA8w"> </span>! je ne l'ai plus<span typeof="mw:DisplaySpace" id="mwA80"> </span>!<br id="mwA84"/></p>
</div></blockquote></div>
<div class="verse-translation-blockquote second"><style data-mw-deduplicate="TemplateStyles:r1152813436" typeof="mw:Extension/templatestyles" about="#mwt374" data-mw='{"name":"templatestyles","attrs":{"src":"Screen reader-only/styles.css"}}'>.mw-parser-output .sr-only{border:0;clip:rect(0,0,0,0);clip-path:polygon(0px 0px,0px 0px,0px 0px);height:1px;margin:-1px;overflow:hidden;padding:0;position:absolute;width:1px;white-space:nowrap}</style><span class="sr-only">Translation:</span><blockquote><div style="font-style:roman;text-align:left" lang="en" class="poem" typeof="mw:Extension/poem" about="#mwt375" id="mwA88" data-mw='{"name":"poem","attrs":{"style":"font-style:roman;text-align:left","lang":"en"},"body":{"extsrc":"Alas! My envious eye, turning toward the past,\nWithout anything else down here able to console me,\nI look continually at that moment of my life\nWhere I saw her spread her wings and fly away!\n\nI will see that instant until I die,\nThat instant, nothing beyond!\nWhere I cried: \"The child I had a short time ago,\nWhat! I have her no more!\"\n"}}'><p id="mwA9A">Alas! My envious eye, turning toward the past,<br id="mwA9E"/>
Without anything else down here able to console me,<br id="mwA9I"/>
I look continually at that moment of my life<br id="mwA9M"/>
Where I saw her spread her wings and fly away!<br id="mwA9Q"/>
<br id="mwA9U"/>
I will see that instant until I die,<br id="mwA9Y"/>
That instant, nothing beyond!<br id="mwA9c"/>
Where I cried: "The child I had a short time ago,<br id="mwA9g"/>
What! I have her no more!"</p></div></blockquote></div>
</div>

<p id="mwA9k">He wrote many poems afterward about his daughter's life and death. His most famous poem is "Demain, dès l'aube" (Tomorrow, at Dawn), in which he describes visiting her grave. Hugo ultimately outlived four of his five children, with the exception of his youngest, Adèle.</p>

</section></section><section data-mw-section-id="12" id="mwA9o" aria-labelledby="Exile"><div class="mw-heading mw-heading3"><h3 id="Exile">Exile</h3><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=Victor_Hugo&amp;action=edit&amp;section=12" title="Edit section: Exile"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<span class="mw-empty-elt" about="#mwt376" typeof="mw:Transclusion" id="mwA9s" data-mw='{"parts":[{"template":{"target":{"wt":"Unreferenced section","href":"./Template:Unreferenced_section"},"params":{"date":{"wt":"June 2023"}},"i":0}}]}'><style data-mw-deduplicate="TemplateStyles:r1364180890" typeof="mw:Extension/templatestyles" about="#mwt377" data-mw='{"name":"templatestyles","attrs":{"src":"Module:Message box/ambox.css"},"body":{"extsrc":""}}'>.mw-parser-output .ambox{border:1px solid #a2a9b1;border-left:10px solid #36c;background-color:#fbfbfb;box-sizing:border-box}.mw-parser-output .ambox+.mw-empty-elt+.ambox,.mw-parser-output .ambox+.mw-empty-elt+.mw-empty-elt+.ambox,.mw-parser-output .ambox+.mw-empty-elt+.mw-empty-elt+.mw-empty-elt+.ambox{margin-top:-1px}html body.mediawiki .mw-parser-output .ambox.mbox-small-left{margin:4px 1em 4px 0;overflow:hidden;width:238px;border-collapse:collapse;font-size:88%;line-height:1.25em}.mw-parser-output .ambox-speedy{border-left:10px solid #b32424;background-color:#fee7e6}.mw-parser-output .ambox-delete{border-left:10px solid #b32424}.mw-parser-output .ambox-content{border-left:10px solid #f28500}.mw-parser-output .ambox-style{border-left:10px solid #fc3}.mw-parser-output .ambox-move{border-left:10px solid #9932cc}.mw-parser-output .ambox-protection{border-left:10px solid #a2a9b1}.mw-parser-output .ambox .mbox-text{border:none;padding:0.25em 0.5em;width:100%}.mw-parser-output .ambox .mbox-image{border:none;padding:2px 0 2px 0.5em;text-align:center}.mw-parser-output .ambox .mbox-imageright{border:none;padding:2px 0.5em 2px 0;text-align:center}.mw-parser-output .ambox .mbox-empty-cell{border:none;padding:0;width:1px}.mw-parser-output .ambox .mbox-image-div{width:52px}@media(min-width:720px){.mw-parser-output .ambox{margin:0 10%}}@media print{body.ns-0 .mw-parser-output .ambox{display:none!important}}</style></span><table class="box-Unreferenced_section plainlinks metadata ambox ambox-content ambox-Unreferenced" role="presentation" about="#mwt376"><tbody><tr><td class="mbox-image"><div class="mbox-image-div"><span typeof="mw:File"><a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/File:Question_book-new.svg" class="mw-file-description"><img alt="icon" resource="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/File:Question_book-new.svg" src="//upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/en/thumb/9/99/Question_book-new.svg/60px-Question_book-new.svg.png" decoding="async" data-file-width="512" data-file-height="399" data-file-type="drawing" height="39" width="50" srcset="//upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/en/thumb/9/99/Question_book-new.svg/120px-Question_book-new.svg.png 2x" class="mw-file-element"/></a></span></div></td><td class="mbox-text"><div class="mbox-text-span">This section <b>does not <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Wikipedia:Citing_sources" title="Wikipedia:Citing sources">cite</a> any <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Wikipedia:Verifiability" title="Wikipedia:Verifiability">sources</a></b>.<span class="hide-when-compact"> Please help <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Special:EditPage/Victor_Hugo" title="Special:EditPage/Victor Hugo">improve this section</a> by <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Help:Referencing_for_beginners" title="Help:Referencing for beginners">adding citations to reliable sources</a>. Unsourced material may be challenged and <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Wikipedia:Verifiability#Burden_of_evidence" title="Wikipedia:Verifiability">removed</a>.</span>  <span class="date-container"><i>(<span class="date">June 2023</span>)</i></span><span class="hide-when-compact"><i> (<small><a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Help:Maintenance_template_removal" title="Help:Maintenance template removal">Learn how and when to remove this message</a></small>)</i></span></div></td></tr></tbody></table><span class="mw-empty-elt" about="#mwt376"><link rel="mw:PageProp/Category" href="./Category:Articles_needing_additional_references_from_June_2023"/><link rel="mw:PageProp/Category" href="./Category:All_articles_needing_additional_references"/></span>
<p id="mwA9w">Hugo decided to live in exile after <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Napoleon_III" title="Napoleon III" id="mwA90">Napoleon III</a>'s <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/French_coup_d'état_of_1851" title="French coup d'état of 1851" class="mw-redirect" id="mwA94">coup d'état at the end of 1851</a>. After leaving France, Hugo lived in Brussels briefly in 1851, and then moved to the <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Channel_Islands" title="Channel Islands" id="mwA98">Channel Islands</a>, first to Jersey (1852–1855) and then to the smaller island of Guernsey in 1855, where he stayed until Napoleon III's fall from power in 1870. Although Napoleon III proclaimed a general amnesty in 1859, under which Hugo could have safely returned to France, the author stayed in exile, only returning when Napoleon III was removed from power by the creation of the <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/French_Third_Republic" title="French Third Republic" id="mwA-A">French Third Republic</a> in 1870, as a result of the French defeat at the <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Battle_of_Sedan" title="Battle of Sedan" id="mwA-E">Battle of Sedan</a> in the <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Franco-Prussian_War" title="Franco-Prussian War" id="mwA-I">Franco-Prussian War</a>. After the <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Siege_of_Paris_(1870-71)" title="Siege of Paris (1870-71)" class="mw-redirect" id="mwA-M">Siege of Paris</a> from 1870 to 1871, Hugo lived again in Guernsey from 1872 to 1873, and then finally returned to France for the remainder of his life. In 1871, after the death of his son Charles, Hugo took custody of his grandchildren <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Jeanne_Hugo" title="Jeanne Hugo" id="mwA-Q">Jeanne</a> and Georges-Victor.</p>

</section><section data-mw-section-id="13" id="mwA-U" aria-labelledby="Other_relationships"><div class="mw-heading mw-heading3"><h3 id="Other_relationships">Other relationships</h3><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=Victor_Hugo&amp;action=edit&amp;section=13" title="Edit section: Other relationships"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<section data-mw-section-id="14" id="mwA-Y" aria-labelledby="Juliette_Drouet"><div class="mw-heading mw-heading4"><h4 id="Juliette_Drouet">Juliette Drouet</h4><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=Victor_Hugo&amp;action=edit&amp;section=14" title="Edit section: Juliette Drouet"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<figure class="mw-default-size mw-halign-right" typeof="mw:File/Thumb" id="mwA-c"><a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/File:Maison_de_Victor_Hugo_Juliette_Drouet_Champmartin_27122012.jpg" class="mw-file-description" id="mwA-g"><img resource="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/File:Maison_de_Victor_Hugo_Juliette_Drouet_Champmartin_27122012.jpg" src="//upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/9/96/Maison_de_Victor_Hugo_Juliette_Drouet_Champmartin_27122012.jpg/250px-Maison_de_Victor_Hugo_Juliette_Drouet_Champmartin_27122012.jpg" decoding="async" data-file-width="1203" data-file-height="1465" data-file-type="bitmap" height="304" width="250" srcset="//upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/9/96/Maison_de_Victor_Hugo_Juliette_Drouet_Champmartin_27122012.jpg/500px-Maison_de_Victor_Hugo_Juliette_Drouet_Champmartin_27122012.jpg 2x" class="mw-file-element" id="mwA-k"/></a><figcaption id="mwA-o">Juliette Drouet</figcaption></figure>
<p id="mwA-s">From February 1833 until her death in 1883, <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Juliette_Drouet" title="Juliette Drouet" id="mwA-w">Juliette Drouet</a> devoted her whole life to Victor Hugo, who never married her even after his wife died in 1868. He took her on his numerous trips and she followed him in exile on <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Guernsey" title="Guernsey" id="mwA-0">Guernsey</a>.<sup about="#mwt380" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-83" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-83"}}'><a href="#cite_note-83" id="mwA-4"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwA-8"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwA_A">[</span>82<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwA_E">]</span></span></a></sup> There Hugo rented a house for her near <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hauteville_House" title="Hauteville House" id="mwA_I">Hauteville House</a>, his family home. She wrote some 20,000 letters in which she expressed her passion or vented her jealousy on her womanizing lover.<sup about="#mwt383" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-84" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-84"}}'><a href="#cite_note-84" id="mwA_M"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwA_Q"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwA_U">[</span>83<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwA_Y">]</span></span></a></sup>
On 25 September 1870 during the <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Siege_of_Paris_(1870–71)" title="Siege of Paris (1870–71)" class="mw-redirect" id="mwA_c">Siege of Paris</a> (19 September 1870 – 28 January 1871) Hugo feared the worst. He left his children a note reading as follows:</p>

<p id="mwA_g">"J.D.
She saved my life in December 1851. For me she underwent exile. Never has her soul forsaken mine. Let those who have loved me love her. Let those who have loved me respect her.
She is my widow."
V.H.<sup about="#mwt386" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-85" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-85"}}'><a href="#cite_note-85" id="mwA_k"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwA_o"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwA_s">[</span>84<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwA_w">]</span></span></a></sup></p>

</section><section data-mw-section-id="15" id="mwA_0" aria-labelledby="Léonie_d'Aunet"><div class="mw-heading mw-heading4"><h4 id="Léonie_d'Aunet"><span id="L.C3.A9onie_d.27Aunet" typeof="mw:FallbackId"></span>Léonie d'Aunet</h4><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=Victor_Hugo&amp;action=edit&amp;section=15" title="Edit section: Léonie d'Aunet"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<p id="mwA_4">For more than seven years, Léonie d'Aunet, who was married, was in a relationship with Hugo. Both were caught committing adultery on 5 July 1845. Hugo, who had been a Member of the <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Chamber_of_Peers_(France)" title="Chamber of Peers (France)" id="mwA_8">Chamber of Peers</a> since April, avoided a custodial sentence whereas his mistress had to spend two months in prison and six in a convent. Many years after their separation, Hugo  supported her financially.<sup about="#mwt389" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-86" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-86"}}'><a href="#cite_note-86" id="mwBAA"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwBAE"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwBAI">[</span>85<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwBAM">]</span></span></a></sup></p>

</section><section data-mw-section-id="16" id="mwBAQ" aria-labelledby="Others"><div class="mw-heading mw-heading4"><h4 id="Others">Others</h4><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=Victor_Hugo&amp;action=edit&amp;section=16" title="Edit section: Others"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<p id="mwBAU">Hugo had lovers until a few weeks before his death. He sought a wide variety of women of all ages, whether courtesans, actresses, prostitutes, admirers, servants or revolutionaries like <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Louise_Michel" title="Louise Michel" id="mwBAY">Louise Michel</a><sup about="#mwt392" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-87" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-87"}}'><a href="#cite_note-87" id="mwBAc"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwBAg"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwBAk">[</span>86<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwBAo">]</span></span></a></sup> for sex. He systematically reported his casual affairs using his own code, as <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Samuel_Pepys" title="Samuel Pepys" id="mwBAs">Samuel Pepys</a> did, to make sure they would remain secret. For instance, he resorted to Latin abbreviations (<i id="mwBAw">osc.</i> for kisses) or to Spanish (<i id="mwBA0">Misma. Mismas cosas</i>: The same. Same things). Homophones are frequent: <i id="mwBA4">Seins</i> (Breasts) becomes Saint; <i id="mwBA8">Poële</i> (Stove) actually refers to <i id="mwBBA">Poils</i> (Pubic hair). Analogy also enabled him to conceal the real meaning: A woman's <i id="mwBBE">Suisses</i> (Swiss) are her breasts—because Switzerland is renowned for its milk. After a rendezvous with a young woman named <i id="mwBBI">Laetitia</i> he would write <i id="mwBBM">Joie</i> (Happiness) in his diary. If he added <i id="mwBBQ">t.n.</i> (<i id="mwBBU">toute nue</i>) he meant she stripped naked in front of him. The initials <i id="mwBBY">S.B.</i> discovered in November 1875 may refer to <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Sarah_Bernhardt" title="Sarah Bernhardt" id="mwBBc">Sarah Bernhardt</a>.<sup about="#mwt395" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-88" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-88"}}'><a href="#cite_note-88" id="mwBBg"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwBBk"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwBBo">[</span>87<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwBBs">]</span></span></a></sup></p>

</section></section></section><section data-mw-section-id="17" id="mwBBw" aria-labelledby="Nicknames"><div class="mw-heading mw-heading2"><h2 id="Nicknames">Nicknames</h2><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=Victor_Hugo&amp;action=edit&amp;section=17" title="Edit section: Nicknames"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div><p id="mwBB0">
Victor Hugo acquired several nicknames. The first one originated from his own description of <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/William_Shakespeare" title="William Shakespeare" id="mwBB4">Shakespeare</a>; in the introduction to his essay <i id="mwBB8"><a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/William_Shakespeare_(essay)" title="William Shakespeare (essay)" id="mwBCA">William Shakespeare</a></i>, he tried to delve into what constituted the genius of certain artists and described Shakespeare as an <i id="mwBCE">Homme Océan</i> ("Ocean Man"):<sup about="#mwt398" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-:0_89-0" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":":0"},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-:0-89"}}'><a href="#cite_note-:0-89" id="mwBCI"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwBCM"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwBCQ">[</span>88<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwBCU">]</span></span></a></sup><sup about="#mwt401" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-90" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-90"}}'><a href="#cite_note-90" id="mwBCY"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwBCc"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwBCg">[</span>89<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwBCk">]</span></span></a></sup><sup about="#mwt404" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-91" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-91"}}'><a href="#cite_note-91" id="mwBCo"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwBCs"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwBCw">[</span>90<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwBC0">]</span></span></a></sup></p><blockquote id="mwBC4"><p id="mwBC8">There were indeed ocean men.<br id="mwBDA"/>[...] all of this could exist within a mind, and then that mind is called genius, and you have <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Aeschylus" title="Aeschylus" id="mwBDE">Aeschylus</a>, you have <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Isaiah" title="Isaiah" id="mwBDI">Isaiah</a>, you have <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Juvenal" title="Juvenal" id="mwBDM">Juvenal</a>, you have <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Dante_Alighieri" title="Dante Alighieri" id="mwBDQ">Dante</a>, you have <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Michelangelo" title="Michelangelo" id="mwBDU">Michelangelo</a>, you have Shakespeare, and it is the same thing to gaze upon these souls or to gaze upon the Ocean.</p></blockquote><p id="mwBDY">During his lifetime and in the 1870s, the expression to describe him entered popular culture.<sup about="#mwt407" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-:1_92-0" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":":1"},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-:1-92"}}'><a href="#cite_note-:1-92" id="mwBDc"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwBDg"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwBDk">[</span>91<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwBDo">]</span></span></a></sup><sup about="#mwt410" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-93" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-93"}}'><a href="#cite_note-93" id="mwBDs"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwBDw"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwBD0">[</span>92<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwBD4">]</span></span></a></sup> It was later adopted by authors and literary critics, including Joseph Serre, who identified it with an expression he himself coined.<sup about="#mwt413" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-94" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-94"}}'><a href="#cite_note-94" id="mwBD8"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwBEA"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwBEE">[</span>93<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwBEI">]</span></span></a></sup><sup about="#mwt416" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-95" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-95"}}'><a href="#cite_note-95" id="mwBEM"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwBEQ"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwBEU">[</span>94<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwBEY">]</span></span></a></sup> This nickname became widespread during the 20th century and was used by the <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Bibliothèque_nationale_de_France" title="Bibliothèque nationale de France" id="mwBEc">National Library of France</a> when organizing an exhibition about him.<sup about="#mwt417" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-:0_89-1" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":":0"}}'><a href="#cite_note-:0-89" id="mwBEg"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwBEk"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwBEo">[</span>88<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwBEs">]</span></span></a></sup> Since then, it has been frequently used in research and literary critics.<sup about="#mwt420" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-96" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-96"}}'><a href="#cite_note-96" id="mwBEw"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwBE0"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwBE4">[</span>95<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwBE8">]</span></span></a></sup><sup about="#mwt423" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-97" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-97"}}'><a href="#cite_note-97" id="mwBFA"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwBFE"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwBFI">[</span>96<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwBFM">]</span></span></a></sup><sup about="#mwt426" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-98" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-98"}}'><a href="#cite_note-98" id="mwBFQ"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwBFU"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwBFY">[</span>97<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwBFc">]</span></span></a></sup><sup about="#mwt429" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-99" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-99"}}'><a href="#cite_note-99" id="mwBFg"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwBFk"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwBFo">[</span>98<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwBFs">]</span></span></a></sup> Alongside "Ocean Man," the nickname "Horizon Man" was sometimes used during Hugo's life.<sup about="#mwt430" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-:1_92-1" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":":1"}}'><a href="#cite_note-:1-92" id="mwBFw"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwBF0"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwBF4">[</span>91<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwBF8">]</span></span></a></sup> In the end researchers and literary critics, such as <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Henri_Meschonnic" title="Henri Meschonnic" id="mwBGA">Henri Meschonnic</a>, suggest the  "Century Man" nickname to refer to Hugo.<sup about="#mwt433" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-100" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-100"}}'><a href="#cite_note-100" id="mwBGE"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwBGI"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwBGM">[</span>99<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwBGQ">]</span></span></a></sup></p>

</section><section data-mw-section-id="18" id="mwBGU" aria-labelledby="Memorials"><div class="mw-heading mw-heading2"><h2 id="Memorials">Memorials</h2><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=Victor_Hugo&amp;action=edit&amp;section=18" title="Edit section: Memorials"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<figure class="mw-default-size mw-halign-left" typeof="mw:File/Thumb" id="mwBGY"><a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/File:Victur-Hugo-Ave-Paris-Photo-by-Pejman-Akbarzadeh.JPG" class="mw-file-description" id="mwBGc"><img alt="" resource="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/File:Victur-Hugo-Ave-Paris-Photo-by-Pejman-Akbarzadeh.JPG" src="//upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/5/50/Victur-Hugo-Ave-Paris-Photo-by-Pejman-Akbarzadeh.JPG/250px-Victur-Hugo-Ave-Paris-Photo-by-Pejman-Akbarzadeh.JPG" decoding="async" data-file-width="3456" data-file-height="2304" data-file-type="bitmap" height="167" width="250" srcset="//upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/5/50/Victur-Hugo-Ave-Paris-Photo-by-Pejman-Akbarzadeh.JPG/500px-Victur-Hugo-Ave-Paris-Photo-by-Pejman-Akbarzadeh.JPG 2x" class="mw-file-element" id="mwBGg"/></a><figcaption id="mwBGk"><span title="French-language text" about="#mwt434" typeof="mw:Transclusion" id="mwBGo" data-mw='{"parts":[{"template":{"target":{"wt":"lang","href":"./Template:Lang"},"params":{"1":{"wt":"fr"},"2":{"wt":"[[Avenue Victor-Hugo (Paris)|Avenue Victor-Hugo]]"},"italic":{"wt":"no"}},"i":0}}]}'><span lang="fr" style="font-style: normal;"><a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Avenue_Victor-Hugo_(Paris)" title="Avenue Victor-Hugo (Paris)">Avenue Victor-Hugo</a></span></span><link rel="mw:PageProp/Category" href="./Category:Articles_containing_French-language_text" about="#mwt434" id="mwBGs"/> in Paris.</figcaption></figure>
<figure class="mw-default-size" typeof="mw:File/Thumb" id="mwBGw"><a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/File:Victor_Hugo_Statue.jpg" class="mw-file-description" id="mwBG0"><img alt=" Statue of Victor Hugo in Rome, Italy." resource="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/File:Victor_Hugo_Statue.jpg" src="//upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/6/6c/Victor_Hugo_Statue.jpg/250px-Victor_Hugo_Statue.jpg" decoding="async" data-file-width="1457" data-file-height="3179" data-file-type="bitmap" height="545" width="250" srcset="//upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/6/6c/Victor_Hugo_Statue.jpg/500px-Victor_Hugo_Statue.jpg 2x" class="mw-file-element" id="mwBG4"/></a><figcaption id="mwBG8">Statue of Victor Hugo in Rome, Italy. It is across from the Museo Carlo Bilotti on Viale Fiorello La Guardia.</figcaption></figure>
<p id="mwBHA">His legacy has been honored in many ways, including his portrait being placed on French currency.</p>

<p id="mwBHE">The people of <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Guernsey" title="Guernsey" id="mwBHI">Guernsey</a> erected a statue by sculptor <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Jean_Boucher_(artist)" title="Jean Boucher (artist)" id="mwBHM">Jean Boucher</a> in <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Candie_Gardens" title="Candie Gardens" id="mwBHQ">Candie Gardens</a> (<a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Saint_Peter_Port" title="Saint Peter Port" id="mwBHU">Saint Peter Port</a>) to commemorate his stay in the islands. The City of Paris has preserved his residences <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hauteville_House" title="Hauteville House" id="mwBHY">Hauteville House</a>, <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Guernsey" title="Guernsey" id="mwBHc">Guernsey</a>, and <span title="French-language text" about="#mwt435" typeof="mw:Transclusion" id="mwBHg" data-mw='{"parts":[{"template":{"target":{"wt":"lang","href":"./Template:Lang"},"params":{"1":{"wt":"fr"},"2":{"wt":"6, Place des Vosges"}},"i":0}}]}'><i lang="fr">6, Place des Vosges</i></span><link rel="mw:PageProp/Category" href="./Category:Articles_containing_French-language_text" about="#mwt435" id="mwBHk"/>, Paris, as museums. The house where he stayed in <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Vianden" title="Vianden" id="mwBHo">Vianden</a>, Luxembourg, in 1871 has also become a museum.</p>

<p id="mwBHs">The <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Avenue_Victor-Hugo_(Paris)" title="Avenue Victor-Hugo (Paris)" id="mwBHw">Avenue Victor-Hugo</a> in the <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/16th_arrondissement_of_Paris" title="16th arrondissement of Paris" id="mwBH0">16th arrondissement of Paris</a> bears Hugo's name and links the <span title="French-language text" about="#mwt436" typeof="mw:Transclusion" id="mwBH4" data-mw='{"parts":[{"template":{"target":{"wt":"lang","href":"./Template:Lang"},"params":{"1":{"wt":"fr"},"2":{"wt":"[[Place de l&apos;Étoile]]"},"italic":{"wt":"no"}},"i":0}}]}'><span lang="fr" style="font-style: normal;"><a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Place_de_l'Étoile" title="Place de l'Étoile" class="mw-redirect">Place de l'Étoile</a></span></span><link rel="mw:PageProp/Category" href="./Category:Articles_containing_French-language_text" about="#mwt436" id="mwBH8"/> to the vicinity of the <span title="French-language text" about="#mwt437" typeof="mw:Transclusion" id="mwBIA" data-mw='{"parts":[{"template":{"target":{"wt":"lang","href":"./Template:Lang"},"params":{"1":{"wt":"fr"},"2":{"wt":"[[Bois de Boulogne]]"},"italic":{"wt":"no"}},"i":0}}]}'><span lang="fr" style="font-style: normal;"><a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Bois_de_Boulogne" title="Bois de Boulogne">Bois de Boulogne</a></span></span><link rel="mw:PageProp/Category" href="./Category:Articles_containing_French-language_text" about="#mwt437" id="mwBIE"/> by way of the <span title="French-language text" about="#mwt438" typeof="mw:Transclusion" id="mwBII" data-mw='{"parts":[{"template":{"target":{"wt":"lang","href":"./Template:Lang"},"params":{"1":{"wt":"fr"},"2":{"wt":"Place Victor-Hugo"},"italic":{"wt":"no"}},"i":0}}]}'><span lang="fr" style="font-style: normal;">Place Victor-Hugo</span></span><link rel="mw:PageProp/Category" href="./Category:Articles_containing_French-language_text" about="#mwt438" id="mwBIM"/>. This square is served by a <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Paris_Metro" title="Paris Metro" id="mwBIQ">Paris Metro</a> <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Victor_Hugo_station" title="Victor Hugo station" id="mwBIU">stop</a> also named in his honour. In the town of <span title="French-language text" about="#mwt439" typeof="mw:Transclusion" id="mwBIY" data-mw='{"parts":[{"template":{"target":{"wt":"lang","href":"./Template:Lang"},"params":{"1":{"wt":"fr"},"2":{"wt":"[[Béziers]]"},"italic":{"wt":"no"}},"i":0}}]}'><span lang="fr" style="font-style: normal;"><a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Béziers" title="Béziers">Béziers</a></span></span><link rel="mw:PageProp/Category" href="./Category:Articles_containing_French-language_text" about="#mwt439" id="mwBIc"/> there is a main street, a school, hospital and several cafés named after Hugo, and a number of streets and avenues throughout France are named after him. The  <span title="French-language text" about="#mwt440" typeof="mw:Transclusion" id="mwBIg" data-mw='{"parts":[{"template":{"target":{"wt":"lang","href":"./Template:Lang"},"params":{"1":{"wt":"fr"},"2":{"wt":"Lycée Victor Hugo"},"italic":{"wt":"no"}},"i":0}}]}'><span lang="fr" style="font-style: normal;">Lycée Victor Hugo</span></span><link rel="mw:PageProp/Category" href="./Category:Articles_containing_French-language_text" about="#mwt440" id="mwBIk"/> school was founded in his town of birth, <span title="French-language text" about="#mwt441" typeof="mw:Transclusion" id="mwBIo" data-mw='{"parts":[{"template":{"target":{"wt":"lang","href":"./Template:Lang"},"params":{"1":{"wt":"fr"},"2":{"wt":"[[Besançon]]"},"italic":{"wt":"no"}},"i":0}}]}'><span lang="fr" style="font-style: normal;"><a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Besançon" title="Besançon">Besançon</a></span></span><link rel="mw:PageProp/Category" href="./Category:Articles_containing_French-language_text" about="#mwt441" id="mwBIs"/> in France. <span title="French-language text" about="#mwt442" typeof="mw:Transclusion" id="mwBIw" data-mw='{"parts":[{"template":{"target":{"wt":"lang","href":"./Template:Lang"},"params":{"1":{"wt":"fr"},"2":{"wt":"Avenue Victor-Hugo"},"italic":{"wt":"no"}},"i":0}}]}'><span lang="fr" style="font-style: normal;">Avenue Victor-Hugo</span></span><link rel="mw:PageProp/Category" href="./Category:Articles_containing_French-language_text" about="#mwt442" id="mwBI0"/>, located in <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Shawinigan" title="Shawinigan" id="mwBI4">Shawinigan</a>, Quebec, was named in honour of him. A street in San Francisco, Hugo Street, is named after him.<sup about="#mwt443" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-101" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-101"}}'><a href="#cite_note-101" id="mwBI8"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwBJA"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwBJE">[</span>100<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwBJI">]</span></span></a></sup></p>
<figure class="mw-default-size mw-halign-left" typeof="mw:File/Thumb" id="mwBJM"><a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/File:France_5_Nouveaux_Francs_1959._VF-_Banknote.jpg" class="mw-file-description" id="mwBJQ"><img alt="" resource="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/File:France_5_Nouveaux_Francs_1959._VF-_Banknote.jpg" src="//upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/c/c6/France_5_Nouveaux_Francs_1959._VF-_Banknote.jpg/250px-France_5_Nouveaux_Francs_1959._VF-_Banknote.jpg" decoding="async" data-file-width="1177" data-file-height="1280" data-file-type="bitmap" height="272" width="250" srcset="//upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/c/c6/France_5_Nouveaux_Francs_1959._VF-_Banknote.jpg/500px-France_5_Nouveaux_Francs_1959._VF-_Banknote.jpg 2x" class="mw-file-element" id="mwBJU"/></a><figcaption id="mwBJY">A 1959 French banknote featuring Hugo</figcaption></figure>
<p id="mwBJc">In the city of <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Avellino" title="Avellino" id="mwBJg">Avellino</a>, Italy, Victor Hugo briefly stayed in what is now known as <span title="Italian-language text" about="#mwt444" typeof="mw:Transclusion" id="mwBJk" data-mw='{"parts":[{"template":{"target":{"wt":"lang","href":"./Template:Lang"},"params":{"1":{"wt":"it"},"2":{"wt":"Il Palazzo Culturale"},"nocat":{"wt":"y"}},"i":0}}]}'><i lang="it">Il Palazzo Culturale</i></span> when reuniting with his father, <span title="French-language text" about="#mwt445" typeof="mw:Transclusion" id="mwBJo" data-mw='{"parts":[{"template":{"target":{"wt":"lang","href":"./Template:Lang"},"params":{"1":{"wt":"fr"},"2":{"wt":"Leopold Sigisbert Hugo"}},"i":0}}]}'><i lang="fr">Leopold Sigisbert Hugo</i></span><link rel="mw:PageProp/Category" href="./Category:Articles_containing_French-language_text" about="#mwt445" id="mwBJs"/>, in 1808. Hugo would later write about his brief stay here, quoting <span title="French-language text" about="#mwt446" typeof="mw:Transclusion" id="mwBJw" data-mw='{"parts":[{"template":{"target":{"wt":"lang","href":"./Template:Lang"},"params":{"1":{"wt":"fr"},"2":{"wt":"\"C&apos;était un palais de marbre...\""},"italic":{"wt":"no"}},"i":0}}]}'><span lang="fr" style="font-style: normal;">"C'était un palais de marbre..."</span></span><link rel="mw:PageProp/Category" href="./Category:Articles_containing_French-language_text" about="#mwt446" id="mwBJ0"/> ("It was a palace of <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Marble" title="Marble" id="mwBJ4">marble</a>").</p>

<p id="mwBJ8">There is a statue of Hugo across from the <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Museo_Carlo_Bilotti?action=edit&amp;redlink=1" title="Museo Carlo Bilotti (page does not exist)" about="#mwt447" typeof="mw:Transclusion mw:LocalizedAttrs" class="new" id="mwBKA" data-mw='{"parts":[{"template":{"target":{"wt":"ill","href":"./Template:Ill"},"params":{"1":{"wt":"Museo Carlo Bilotti"},"2":{"wt":"it"}},"i":0}}]}' data-mw-i18n='{"title":{"lang":"x-page","key":"red-link-title","params":["Museo Carlo Bilotti"]}}'>Museo Carlo Bilotti</a><span class="noprint" style="font-size:85%; font-style: normal; " about="#mwt447" id="mwBKE"><span typeof="mw:Entity"> </span><span typeof="mw:Entity">[</span><a rel="mw:WikiLink/Interwiki" href="https://it.wikipedia.org/wiki/Museo%20Carlo%20Bilotti" title="it:Museo Carlo Bilotti" class="extiw">it</a><span typeof="mw:Entity">]</span></span> in Rome, Italy.</p>

<p id="mwBKI">Victor Hugo is the namesake of the city of <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hugoton,_Kansas" title="Hugoton, Kansas" id="mwBKM">Hugoton, Kansas</a>.<sup about="#mwt450" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-102" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-102"}}'><a href="#cite_note-102" id="mwBKQ"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwBKU"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwBKY">[</span>101<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwBKc">]</span></span></a></sup>
Hugo, MN boasts Victor Hugo Blvd, Victor Square park, Victor Gardens, Victor path, and Victor Hugo Clocktower.
In <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Havana" title="Havana" id="mwBKg">Havana</a>, Cuba, there is a park named after him.</p>
<figure class="mw-default-size" typeof="mw:File/Thumb" id="mwBKk"><a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/File:Stamp_of_USSR_1683.jpg" class="mw-file-description" id="mwBKo"><img resource="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/File:Stamp_of_USSR_1683.jpg" src="//upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/a/a5/Stamp_of_USSR_1683.jpg/250px-Stamp_of_USSR_1683.jpg" decoding="async" data-file-width="1468" data-file-height="2003" data-file-type="bitmap" height="341" width="250" srcset="//upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/a/a5/Stamp_of_USSR_1683.jpg/500px-Stamp_of_USSR_1683.jpg 2x" class="mw-file-element" id="mwBKs"/></a><figcaption id="mwBKw">1952 Soviet stamp in Hugo's honour</figcaption></figure>
<p id="mwBK0">A bust of Hugo stands near the entrance of the <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Old_Summer_Palace" title="Old Summer Palace" id="mwBK4">Old Summer Palace</a> in Beijing marking his criticism of its destruction by Anglo-French forces during the <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Second_Opium_War" title="Second Opium War" id="mwBK8">Second Opium War</a>.</p>

<p id="mwBLA">A <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Mosaic" title="Mosaic" id="mwBLE">mosaic</a> commemorating Hugo is located on the ceiling of the <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Thomas_Jefferson_Building" title="Thomas Jefferson Building" id="mwBLI">Thomas Jefferson Building</a> of the <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Library_of_Congress" title="Library of Congress" id="mwBLM">Library of Congress</a>.</p>

<p id="mwBLQ">The <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/London_and_North_Western_Railway" title="London and North Western Railway" id="mwBLU">London and North Western Railway</a> named a 'Prince of Wales' Class 4-6-0 No 1134 after Hugo. <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/British_Rail" title="British Rail" id="mwBLY">British Railways</a> perpetuated this memorial, naming Class 92 Electric Unit 92001 after him.</p>

<figure class="mw-default-size" typeof="mw:File/Thumb" id="mwBLc"><a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/File:Bức_Họa_Tam_Thánh_012014.JPG" class="mw-file-description" id="mwBLg"><img resource="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/File:Bức_Họa_Tam_Thánh_012014.JPG" src="//upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/d/dd/B%E1%BB%A9c_H%E1%BB%8Da_Tam_Th%C3%A1nh_012014.JPG/250px-B%E1%BB%A9c_H%E1%BB%8Da_Tam_Th%C3%A1nh_012014.JPG" decoding="async" data-file-width="2543" data-file-height="3471" data-file-type="bitmap" height="341" width="250" srcset="//upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/d/dd/B%E1%BB%A9c_H%E1%BB%8Da_Tam_Th%C3%A1nh_012014.JPG/500px-B%E1%BB%A9c_H%E1%BB%8Da_Tam_Th%C3%A1nh_012014.JPG 2x" class="mw-file-element" id="mwBLk"/></a><figcaption id="mwBLo">Victor Hugo, along with <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Sun_Yat-Sen" title="Sun Yat-Sen" class="mw-redirect" id="mwBLs">Sun Yat-Sen</a> and <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Nguyễn_Bỉnh_Khiêm" title="Nguyễn Bỉnh Khiêm" id="mwBLw">Nguyễn Bỉnh Khiêm</a>, is venerated in <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Caodaism" title="Caodaism" id="mwBL0">Caodaism</a></figcaption></figure>

<p id="mwBL4">Hugo is venerated as a <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Saint" title="Saint" id="mwBL8">saint</a> in the religion of <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Caodaism" title="Caodaism" id="mwBMA">Caodaism</a>, which was founded in Vietnam in 1926.<sup about="#mwt454" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-103" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-103"}}'><a href="#cite_note-103" id="mwBME"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwBMI"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwBMM">[</span>102<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwBMQ">]</span></span></a></sup><sup about="#mwt457" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-104" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-104"}}'><a href="#cite_note-104" id="mwBMU"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwBMY"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwBMc">[</span>103<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwBMg">]</span></span></a></sup><sup about="#mwt460" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-105" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-105"}}'><a href="#cite_note-105" id="mwBMk"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwBMo"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwBMs">[</span>104<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwBMw">]</span></span></a></sup><sup about="#mwt463" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-106" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-106"}}'><a href="#cite_note-106" id="mwBM0"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwBM4"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwBM8">[</span>105<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwBNA">]</span></span></a></sup></p>

<p id="mwBNE">A <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hugo_(crater)" title="Hugo (crater)" id="mwBNI">crater</a> on the planet <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Mercury_(planet)" title="Mercury (planet)" id="mwBNM">Mercury</a> is named after him.</p>

</section><section data-mw-section-id="19" id="mwBNQ" aria-labelledby="Works"><div class="mw-heading mw-heading2"><h2 id="Works">Works</h2><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=Victor_Hugo&amp;action=edit&amp;section=19" title="Edit section: Works"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>

<section data-mw-section-id="20" id="mwBNw" aria-labelledby="Prose_fiction"><div class="mw-heading mw-heading3"><h3 id="Prose_fiction">Prose fiction</h3><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=Victor_Hugo&amp;action=edit&amp;section=20" title="Edit section: Prose fiction"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<ul id="mwBN0"><li id="mwBN4"><i id="mwBN8"><a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hans_of_Iceland" title="Hans of Iceland" id="mwBOA">Hans of Iceland</a></i> (<i id="mwBOE">Han d'Islande</i> – novella - 1820, novel - 1823, revised until 1833)</li>
<li id="mwBOI"><i id="mwBOM"><a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Bug-Jargal" title="Bug-Jargal" id="mwBOQ">Bug-Jargal</a></i> (short story – 1820, novel – 1826)</li>
<li id="mwBOU"><i id="mwBOY"><a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/The_Last_Day_of_a_Condemned_Man" title="The Last Day of a Condemned Man" id="mwBOc">The Last Day of a Condemned Man</a></i> (<span title="French-language text" about="#mwt464" typeof="mw:Transclusion" id="mwBOg" data-mw='{"parts":[{"template":{"target":{"wt":"lang","href":"./Template:Lang"},"params":{"1":{"wt":"fr"},"2":{"wt":"Le Dernier jour d&apos;un condamné"}},"i":0}}]}'><i lang="fr">Le Dernier jour d'un condamné</i></span><link rel="mw:PageProp/Category" href="./Category:Articles_containing_French-language_text" about="#mwt464" id="mwBOk"/> – 1829) - first prototype for <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Les_Misérables" title="Les Misérables" id="mwBOo">Les Misérables</a></li>
<li id="mwBOs"><i id="mwBOw"><a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/The_Hunchback_of_Notre-Dame" title="The Hunchback of Notre-Dame" id="mwBO0">The Hunchback of Notre-Dame</a></i> (<span title="French-language text" about="#mwt465" typeof="mw:Transclusion" id="mwBO4" data-mw='{"parts":[{"template":{"target":{"wt":"lang","href":"./Template:Lang"},"params":{"1":{"wt":"fr"},"2":{"wt":"Notre-Dame de Paris"}},"i":0}}]}'><i lang="fr">Notre-Dame de Paris</i></span><link rel="mw:PageProp/Category" href="./Category:Articles_containing_French-language_text" about="#mwt465" id="mwBO8"/> – 1831, definitive unabridged edition: 1832)
<ul id="mwBPA"><li id="mwBPE"><i id="mwBPI"><a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/La_Esmeralda_(opera)" title="La Esmeralda (opera)" id="mwBPM">La Esmeralda</a></i> (the only libretto of an opera written by Victor Hugo himself) (1836) – the ending was altered for the play</li></ul></li>
<li id="mwBPQ"><i id="mwBPU"><a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Claude_Gueux" title="Claude Gueux" id="mwBPY">Claude Gueux</a></i> (1834) - second prototype for <i id="mwBPc"><a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Les_Misérables" title="Les Misérables" id="mwBPg">Les Misérables</a></i></li>
<li id="mwBPk">The Rhine (<i id="mwBPo"><a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Le_Rhin" title="Le Rhin" id="mwBPs">Le Rhin</a></i> – 1842) - also contains essays and a political manifesto</li>
<li id="mwBPw"><i id="mwBP0"><a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/The_Poor_People" title="The Poor People" id="mwBP4">The Poor People</a></i> (1854) - short story included in <i id="mwBP8"><a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/La_Légende_des_siècles" title="La Légende des siècles" id="mwBQA">La Légende des siècles</a></i>, later rewritten or retold by <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Leo_Tolstoy" title="Leo Tolstoy" id="mwBQE">Leo Tolstoy</a> in <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Russian_language" title="Russian language" id="mwBQI">Russian</a> (1908)</li>
<li id="mwBQM"><span title="French-language text" about="#mwt466" typeof="mw:Transclusion" id="mwBQQ" data-mw='{"parts":[{"template":{"target":{"wt":"lang","href":"./Template:Lang"},"params":{"1":{"wt":"fr"},"2":{"wt":"[[Les Misérables]]"}},"i":0}}]}'><i lang="fr"><a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Les_Misérables" title="Les Misérables">Les Misérables</a></i></span><link rel="mw:PageProp/Category" href="./Category:Articles_containing_French-language_text" about="#mwt466" id="mwBQU"/> (1862) - mega-novel, initially begun as <i id="mwBQY">Les Misères</i>. 7 chapters from Book 7 of Part 3 were excised by <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Albert_Lacroix" title="Albert Lacroix" id="mwBQc">Albert Lacroix</a> at the time of publication. None of the English translations include the excised chapters but some French editions include them as Appendix or in Notes.
<ul id="mwBQg"><li id="mwBQk"><i id="mwBQo">Les Misères</i> (1845-48) - two draft versions of <i id="mwBQs"><a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Les_Misérables" title="Les Misérables" id="mwBQw">Les Misérables</a></i> - not yet translated into English but available online in French</li></ul></li>
<li id="mwBQ0"><i id="mwBQ4"><a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Toilers_of_the_Sea" title="Toilers of the Sea" id="mwBQ8">Toilers of the Sea</a></i> (<span title="French-language text" about="#mwt467" typeof="mw:Transclusion" id="mwBRA" data-mw='{"parts":[{"template":{"target":{"wt":"lang","href":"./Template:Lang"},"params":{"1":{"wt":"fr"},"2":{"wt":"Les Travailleurs de la Mer"}},"i":0}}]}'><i lang="fr">Les Travailleurs de la Mer</i></span><link rel="mw:PageProp/Category" href="./Category:Articles_containing_French-language_text" about="#mwt467" id="mwBRE"/> – 1866)</li>
<li id="mwBRI"><i id="mwBRM"><a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/The_Man_Who_Laughs" title="The Man Who Laughs" id="mwBRQ">The Man Who Laughs</a></i> ( <span title="French-language text" about="#mwt468" typeof="mw:Transclusion" id="mwBRU" data-mw='{"parts":[{"template":{"target":{"wt":"lang","href":"./Template:Lang"},"params":{"1":{"wt":"fr"},"2":{"wt":"L&apos;Homme qui rit"}},"i":0}}]}'><i lang="fr">L'Homme qui rit</i></span><link rel="mw:PageProp/Category" href="./Category:Articles_containing_French-language_text" about="#mwt468" id="mwBRY"/> – 1869)</li>
<li id="mwBRc"><i id="mwBRg"><a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Ninety-Three" title="Ninety-Three" id="mwBRk">Ninety-Three</a></i> (Quatrevingt-treize – 1874)</li></ul>
